id=a4cbe7f91c928b83	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=MERGE	complexity=complex	exec_ms=27056.796	user=BATCH_USER	database=AVIATION_PROD	query_text=MERGE /*+ USE_NL(target source) */ INTO AVIATION.CUSTOMER_SUMMARY target USING ( SELECT c.customer_id, COUNT(DISTINCT o.order_id) as order_count, SUM(o.total_amount) as lifetime_value, AVG(o.total_amount) as avg_order_value, MAX(o.order_date) as last_order_date, MIN(o.order_date) as first_order_date, MONTHS_BETWEEN(SYSDATE, MAX(o.order_date)) as months_since_last_order FROM AVIATION.CUSTOMERS c LEFT JOIN AVIATION.ORDERS o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id AND o.status = 'COMPLETED' AND o.order_date >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -12) WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY c.customer_id ) source ON (target.customer_id = source.customer_id) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET target.order_count = source.order_count, target.lifetime_value = source.lifetime_value, target.avg_order_value = source.avg_order_value, target.last_order_date = source.last_order_date, target.months_since_last_order = source.months_since_last_order, target.last_updated = SYSDATE WHERE target.order_count != source.order_count OR target.lifetime_value != source.lifetime_value WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (customer_id, order_count, lifetime_value, avg_order_value, last_order_date, first_order_date, months_since_last_order, created_date, last_updated) VALUES (source.customer_id, source.order_count, source.lifetime_value, source.avg_order_value, source.last_order_date, source.first_order_date, source.months_since_last_order, SYSDATE, SYSDATE)
id=f62e7771961d3921	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=PLSQL	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=49137.013	user=PROC_USER	database=LOGISTICS_PROD	query_text=DECLARE CURSOR emp_cursor IS SELECT employee_id, salary, department_id FROM LOGISTICS.EMPLOYEES WHERE hire_date >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -12) FOR UPDATE; v_bonus_pct NUMBER; v_total_updated NUMBER := 0; BEGIN FOR emp_rec IN emp_cursor LOOP -- Calculate bonus based on department performance SELECT NVL(dept_performance_pct, 5) INTO v_bonus_pct FROM LOGISTICS.DEPARTMENT_METRICS WHERE department_id = emp_rec.department_id AND metric_year = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE); -- Update employee bonus UPDATE LOGISTICS.EMPLOYEE_BONUSES SET bonus_amount = emp_rec.salary * (v_bonus_pct / 100), calculated_date = SYSDATE WHERE employee_id = emp_rec.employee_id AND bonus_year = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE); v_total_updated := v_total_updated + 1; IF MOD(v_total_updated, 1000) = 0 THEN COMMIT; END IF; END LOOP; COMMIT; DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Total employees updated: ' || v_total_updated); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN ROLLBACK; RAISE; END;

id=29556f9a249f4e5c	db_engine=MySQL	query_type=SELECT	complexity=complex	exec_ms=7890.0	user=nan	database=nan	query_text=SELECT t.priority, t.category, t.status, COUNT(*) as ticket_count, AVG(TIMESTAMPDIFF(HOUR, t.created_at, t.first_response_at)) as avg_first_response_hours, AVG(TIMESTAMPDIFF(HOUR, t.created_at, t.resolved_at)) as avg_resolution_hours, COUNT(CASE WHEN t.satisfaction_rating >= 4 THEN 1 END) as satisfied_customers FROM support_tickets t WHERE t.created_at >= DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 30 DAY) GROUP BY t.priority, t.category, t.status ORDER BY ticket_count DESC
id=403bfdb7605ffc2e	db_engine=Cassandra	query_type=UPDATE	complexity=complex	exec_ms=217.39	user=inventory_system	database=security_cluster	query_text=UPDATE security.inventory SET stock_quantity = stock_quantity - 5, last_updated = toTimestamp(now()) WHERE warehouse_id = 'WH0035' AND product_id = 'PROD00000035' IF stock_quantity >= 5;
id=c52aafa3f3379a52	db_engine=MySQL	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=14678.901	user=nan	database=nan	query_text=SELECT e.event_id, e.event_name, e.event_date, v.venue_name, cs.stand_id, cs.stand_location, cs.stand_type, COUNT(DISTINCT s.sale_id) as transaction_count, SUM(si.quantity) as items_sold, SUM(si.quantity * si.unit_price) as total_revenue, AVG(s.transaction_total) as avg_transaction_value, SUM(CASE WHEN si.item_category = 'food' THEN si.quantity * si.unit_price ELSE 0 END) as food_revenue, SUM(CASE WHEN si.item_category = 'beverage' THEN si.quantity * si.unit_price ELSE 0 END) as beverage_revenue, SUM(CASE WHEN si.item_category = 'merchandise' THEN si.quantity * si.unit_price ELSE 0 END) as merch_revenue, (SUM(si.quantity * si.unit_price) - SUM(si.quantity * inv.cost_per_unit)) as gross_profit, ((SUM(si.quantity * si.unit_price) - SUM(si.quantity * inv.cost_per_unit)) / SUM(si.quantity * si.unit_price) * 100) as profit_margin_pct, MAX(s.sale_timestamp) as last_sale_time, TIMESTAMPDIFF(MINUTE, e.event_time, MAX(s.sale_timestamp)) as minutes_into_event FROM events e INNER JOIN venues v ON e.venue_id = v.venue_id INNER JOIN concession_stands cs ON v.venue_id = cs.venue_id LEFT JOIN concession_sales s ON cs.stand_id = s.stand_id AND s.event_id = e.event_id LEFT JOIN sale_items si ON s.sale_id = si.sale_id LEFT JOIN inventory inv ON si.item_id = inv.item_id WHERE e.event_date >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 30 DAY) AND e.event_status = 'completed' GROUP BY e.event_id, e.event_name, e.event_date, v.venue_name, cs.stand_id, cs.stand_location, cs.stand_type, e.event_time HAVING transaction_count > 0 ORDER BY total_revenue DESC, profit_margin_pct DESC LIMIT 740

id=af07ba6dee81359a	db_engine=SQL Server	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=15033.23	user=readonly_user	database=banking_db	query_text=SELECT u.user_id, u.username, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM BANKING.TRANSACTIONS t WHERE t.user_id = u.user_id) as transaction_count, (SELECT SUM(amount) FROM BANKING.TRANSACTIONS t WHERE t.user_id = u.user_id AND t.status = 'completed') as total_amount FROM BANKING.USERS u WHERE u.status = 'active' AND u.created_date >= '2024-01-01' ORDER BY total_amount DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 500
id=6902a25fdf348a7d	db_engine=MySQL	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=13567.89	user=nan	database=nan	query_text=SELECT rf.facility_id, rf.facility_name, rf.facility_location, rm.material_type, rm.material_category, SUM(rp.quantity_processed_tons) as total_processed, SUM(rp.quantity_contaminated_tons) as total_contaminated, (SUM(rp.quantity_contaminated_tons) / SUM(rp.quantity_processed_tons) * 100) as contamination_rate_pct, SUM(rp.quantity_baled_tons) as total_baled, COUNT(DISTINCT rp.batch_id) as batch_count, AVG(rp.processing_time_hours) as avg_processing_time, SUM(rs.sale_quantity_tons) as quantity_sold, SUM(rs.sale_amount) as total_revenue, (SUM(rs.sale_amount) / SUM(rs.sale_quantity_tons)) as avg_price_per_ton, SUM(rp.operating_cost) as total_operating_cost, (SUM(rs.sale_amount) - SUM(rp.operating_cost)) as net_profit, ((SUM(rs.sale_amount) - SUM(rp.operating_cost)) / SUM(rs.sale_amount) * 100) as profit_margin_pct FROM recycling_facilities rf INNER JOIN recyclable_materials rm ON rf.facility_id = rm.facility_id LEFT JOIN recycling_processing rp ON rm.material_id = rp.material_id AND rp.processing_date >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 90 DAY) LEFT JOIN recycling_sales rs ON rp.batch_id = rs.batch_id WHERE rf.facility_status = 'operational' GROUP BY rf.facility_id, rf.facility_name, rf.facility_location, rm.material_type, rm.material_category HAVING contamination_rate_pct > 10 OR profit_margin_pct < 15 ORDER BY contamination_rate_pct DESC, profit_margin_pct ASC LIMIT 620

id=2d6e983f0eccdb88	db_engine=SQLite	query_type=UPDATE	complexity=moderate	exec_ms=6583.496	user=sync_service	database=emergency_services_local.db	query_text=UPDATE emergency_services_inventory SET quantity = quantity - ( SELECT SUM(quantity) FROM emergency_services_usage WHERE emergency_services_usage.item_id = emergency_services_inventory.item_id AND usage_date = date('now') AND status = 'confirmed' ), last_updated = datetime('now'), updated_by = 'system' WHERE EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM emergency_services_usage WHERE emergency_services_usage.item_id = emergency_services_inventory.item_id AND usage_date = date('now') AND status = 'confirmed' )
id=afe4bab36c9c9ba7	db_engine=SQL Server	query_type=SELECT	complexity=moderate	exec_ms=15158.1	user=batch_processor	database=healthcare_db	query_text=SELECT c.category_name, COUNT(DISTINCT o.order_id) as total_orders, SUM(oi.quantity * oi.unit_price) as total_revenue, AVG(oi.quantity * oi.unit_price) as avg_order_value FROM HEALTHCARE.ORDERS o INNER JOIN HEALTHCARE.ORDER_ITEMS oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id INNER JOIN HEALTHCARE.PRODUCTS p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id INNER JOIN HEALTHCARE.CATEGORIES c ON p.category_id = c.category_id WHERE o.order_date BETWEEN '2024-01-01' AND '2024-10-06' AND o.status = 'completed' GROUP BY c.category_name HAVING SUM(oi.quantity * oi.unit_price) > 10000 ORDER BY total_revenue DESC LIMIT 50

id=4bedf9077cd5cbb0	db_engine=SQL Server	query_type=SELECT	complexity=complex	exec_ms=13788.0	user=analytics_user	database=agriculture_db	query_text=SELECT u.user_id, u.username, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM AGRICULTURE.TRANSACTIONS t WHERE t.user_id = u.user_id) as transaction_count, (SELECT SUM(amount) FROM AGRICULTURE.TRANSACTIONS t WHERE t.user_id = u.user_id AND t.status = 'completed') as total_amount FROM AGRICULTURE.USERS u WHERE u.status = 'active' AND u.created_date >= '2024-01-01' ORDER BY total_amount DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 500
id=b9160b72af078e17	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=PLSQL	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=41720.997	user=PROC_USER	database=UTILITIES_PROD	query_text=DECLARE CURSOR emp_cursor IS SELECT employee_id, salary, department_id FROM UTILITIES.EMPLOYEES WHERE hire_date >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -12) FOR UPDATE; v_bonus_pct NUMBER; v_total_updated NUMBER := 0; BEGIN FOR emp_rec IN emp_cursor LOOP -- Calculate bonus based on department performance SELECT NVL(dept_performance_pct, 5) INTO v_bonus_pct FROM UTILITIES.DEPARTMENT_METRICS WHERE department_id = emp_rec.department_id AND metric_year = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE); -- Update employee bonus UPDATE UTILITIES.EMPLOYEE_BONUSES SET bonus_amount = emp_rec.salary * (v_bonus_pct / 100), calculated_date = SYSDATE WHERE employee_id = emp_rec.employee_id AND bonus_year = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE); v_total_updated := v_total_updated + 1; IF MOD(v_total_updated, 1000) = 0 THEN COMMIT; END IF; END LOOP; COMMIT; DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Total employees updated: ' || v_total_updated); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN ROLLBACK; RAISE; END;

id=2dc98c798f26f14d	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=22816.18	user=ETL_USER	database=BANKING_DW	query_text=SELECT /*+ PARALLEL(t, 8) FULL(t) */ TO_CHAR(t.transaction_date, 'YYYY-MM') as transaction_month, t.transaction_type, t.account_id, a.account_number, a.account_type, COUNT(*) as transaction_count, SUM(t.amount) as total_amount, AVG(t.amount) as avg_amount, MIN(t.amount) as min_amount, MAX(t.amount) as max_amount, STDDEV(t.amount) as amount_stddev FROM BANKING.TRANSACTIONS PARTITION FOR (TO_DATE('2024-10-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD')) t INNER JOIN BANKING.ACCOUNTS a ON t.account_id = a.account_id WHERE t.transaction_date >= TO_DATE('2024-10-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD') AND t.transaction_date < ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE('2024-10-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD'), 1) AND t.status = 'COMPLETED' GROUP BY TO_CHAR(t.transaction_date, 'YYYY-MM'), t.transaction_type, t.account_id, a.account_number, a.account_type HAVING SUM(t.amount) > 10000 ORDER BY total_amount DESC FETCH FIRST 1000 ROWS ONLY
id=70dfabfbfe0c9ca7	db_engine=MariaDB	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=23494.435	user=catalog_service	database=publishing_catalog	query_text=WITH RECURSIVE hierarchy AS ( SELECT id, parent_id, name, 1 as level FROM publishing_categories WHERE parent_id IS NULL UNION ALL SELECT c.id, c.parent_id, c.name, h.level + 1 FROM publishing_categories c JOIN hierarchy h ON c.parent_id = h.id WHERE h.level < 10 ) SELECT h.id, h.name, h.level, COUNT(i.id) as item_count, SUM(i.amount) as total_value FROM hierarchy h LEFT JOIN publishing_items i ON h.id = i.category_id GROUP BY h.id, h.name, h.level ORDER BY h.level, h.name

id=2cc990b8a1487b9e	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=32505.417	user=ETL_USER	database=UTILITIES_DW	query_text=SELECT /*+ PARALLEL(t, 8) FULL(t) */ TO_CHAR(t.transaction_date, 'YYYY-MM') as transaction_month, t.transaction_type, t.account_id, a.account_number, a.account_type, COUNT(*) as transaction_count, SUM(t.amount) as total_amount, AVG(t.amount) as avg_amount, MIN(t.amount) as min_amount, MAX(t.amount) as max_amount, STDDEV(t.amount) as amount_stddev FROM UTILITIES.TRANSACTIONS PARTITION FOR (TO_DATE('2024-10-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD')) t INNER JOIN UTILITIES.ACCOUNTS a ON t.account_id = a.account_id WHERE t.transaction_date >= TO_DATE('2024-10-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD') AND t.transaction_date < ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE('2024-10-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD'), 1) AND t.status = 'COMPLETED' GROUP BY TO_CHAR(t.transaction_date, 'YYYY-MM'), t.transaction_type, t.account_id, a.account_number, a.account_type HAVING SUM(t.amount) > 10000 ORDER BY total_amount DESC FETCH FIRST 1000 ROWS ONLY
id=dfbd10b92b16ed6f	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=MERGE	complexity=complex	exec_ms=24490.171	user=BATCH_USER	database=HEALTHCARE_PROD	query_text=MERGE /*+ USE_NL(target source) */ INTO HEALTHCARE.CUSTOMER_SUMMARY target USING ( SELECT c.customer_id, COUNT(DISTINCT o.order_id) as order_count, SUM(o.total_amount) as lifetime_value, AVG(o.total_amount) as avg_order_value, MAX(o.order_date) as last_order_date, MIN(o.order_date) as first_order_date, MONTHS_BETWEEN(SYSDATE, MAX(o.order_date)) as months_since_last_order FROM HEALTHCARE.CUSTOMERS c LEFT JOIN HEALTHCARE.ORDERS o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id AND o.status = 'COMPLETED' AND o.order_date >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -12) WHERE c.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY c.customer_id ) source ON (target.customer_id = source.customer_id) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET target.order_count = source.order_count, target.lifetime_value = source.lifetime_value, target.avg_order_value = source.avg_order_value, target.last_order_date = source.last_order_date, target.months_since_last_order = source.months_since_last_order, target.last_updated = SYSDATE WHERE target.order_count != source.order_count OR target.lifetime_value != source.lifetime_value WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (customer_id, order_count, lifetime_value, avg_order_value, last_order_date, first_order_date, months_since_last_order, created_date, last_updated) VALUES (source.customer_id, source.order_count, source.lifetime_value, source.avg_order_value, source.last_order_date, source.first_order_date, source.months_since_last_order, SYSDATE, SYSDATE)

id=470795838361c04a	db_engine=Oracle	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=26052.3	user=REPORT_USER	database=PHARMACEUTICALS_PROD	query_text=SELECT /*+ INDEX(org ORG_HIERARCHY_IDX) */ LEVEL as org_level, SYS_CONNECT_BY_PATH(org_name, ' > ') as org_path, org_id, org_name, parent_org_id, COUNT(DISTINCT e.employee_id) as employee_count, SUM(b.budget_amount) as total_budget, CONNECT_BY_ROOT org_name as root_organization, CONNECT_BY_ISLEAF as is_leaf_node FROM PHARMACEUTICALS.ORGANIZATION_HIERARCHY org LEFT JOIN PHARMACEUTICALS.EMPLOYEES e ON org.org_id = e.org_id LEFT JOIN PHARMACEUTICALS.BUDGETS b ON org.org_id = b.org_id AND b.fiscal_year = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE) WHERE org.status = 'ACTIVE' START WITH parent_org_id IS NULL CONNECT BY PRIOR org_id = parent_org_id ORDER SIBLINGS BY org_name

id=792d90300be7c18d	db_engine=SQL Server	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=19267.002	user=etl_service	database=AviationDW	query_text=WITH SourceData AS ( SELECT ID, Name, Amount, Status, Category, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY Category ORDER BY CreatedDate DESC) as RowNum FROM AviationStaging WHERE ProcessedDate IS NULL ) MERGE AviationTarget AS target USING SourceData AS source ON target.ID = source.ID WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET Name = source.Name, Amount = source.Amount, Status = source.Status WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (ID, Name, Amount, Status, Category) VALUES (source.ID, source.Name, source.Amount, source.Status, source.Category) OUTPUT $action, INSERTED.ID, INSERTED.Name;
id=8e7ccaaa1e333971	db_engine=MySQL	query_type=UPDATE	complexity=moderate	exec_ms=14890.123000000001	user=nan	database=nan	query_text=UPDATE aircraft_maintenance am INNER JOIN maintenance_schedules ms ON am.aircraft_id = ms.aircraft_id INNER JOIN maintenance_tasks mt ON am.maintenance_id = mt.maintenance_id SET am.maintenance_status = CASE WHEN mt.task_status = 'completed' AND ms.schedule_type = 'A-check' THEN 'airworthy' WHEN mt.task_status = 'failed' THEN 'grounded' WHEN mt.task_status = 'in_progress' THEN 'under_maintenance' ELSE am.maintenance_status END, am.next_check_date = CASE WHEN mt.task_status = 'completed' THEN DATE_ADD(CURDATE(), INTERVAL ms.check_interval_days DAY) ELSE am.next_check_date END, am.last_check_date = CASE WHEN mt.task_status = 'completed' THEN CURDATE() ELSE am.last_check_date END, am.flight_hours_since_check = 0, am.last_updated = NOW() WHERE mt.completion_date >= DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 1 DAY) AND mt.processed = 0 AND ms.schedule_type IN ('A-check', 'B-check', 'C-check', 'D-check')

id=70ca2eba262caffd	db_engine=MySQL	query_type=UPDATE	complexity=complex	exec_ms=18345.0	user=nan	database=nan	query_text=UPDATE user_statistics us INNER JOIN ( SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) as order_count, SUM(total_amount) as lifetime_value, MAX(created_at) as last_order_date FROM orders WHERE created_at >= DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 7 DAY) GROUP BY user_id ) recent_orders ON us.user_id = recent_orders.user_id SET us.total_orders = us.total_orders + recent_orders.order_count, us.lifetime_value = us.lifetime_value + recent_orders.lifetime_value, us.last_purchase_date = recent_orders.last_order_date, us.updated_at = NOW()

id=4c8ec7bf3fce3444	db_engine=MySQL	query_type=SELECT	complexity=very_complex	exec_ms=18456.789	user=nan	database=nan	query_text=SELECT sh.ship_id, sh.ship_name, sh.ship_class, sh.build_year, YEAR(CURDATE()) - sh.build_year as ship_age_years, cr.cruise_id, cr.departure_date, cr.return_date, COUNT(DISTINCT mt.task_id) as maintenance_tasks, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN mt.task_priority = 'critical' THEN mt.task_id END) as critical_tasks, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN mt.task_priority = 'high' THEN mt.task_id END) as high_priority_tasks, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN mt.task_status = 'completed' THEN mt.task_id END) as completed_tasks, (COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN mt.task_status = 'completed' THEN mt.task_id END) / COUNT(DISTINCT mt.task_id) * 100) as completion_pct, SUM(mt.estimated_cost) as total_estimated_cost, SUM(CASE WHEN mt.task_status = 'completed' THEN mt.actual_cost ELSE 0 END) as total_actual_cost, COUNT(DISTINCT si.inspection_id) as safety_inspections, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN si.inspection_result = 'pass' THEN si.inspection_id END) as passed_inspections, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN si.inspection_result = 'fail' THEN si.inspection_id END) as failed_inspections, MAX(si.inspection_date) as last_inspection_date, DATEDIFF(CURDATE(), MAX(si.inspection_date)) as days_since_last_inspection FROM ships sh LEFT JOIN cruises cr ON sh.ship_id = cr.ship_id AND cr.cruise_status = 'in_progress' LEFT JOIN maintenance_tasks mt ON sh.ship_id = mt.ship_id AND mt.scheduled_date >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 90 DAY) LEFT JOIN safety_inspections si ON sh.ship_id = si.ship_id AND si.inspection_date >= DATE_SUB(CURDATE(), INTERVAL 180 DAY) WHERE sh.ship_status IN ('operational', 'in_service') GROUP BY sh.ship_id, sh.ship_name, sh.ship_class, sh.build_year, cr.cruise_id, cr.departure_date, cr.return_date HAVING critical_tasks > 0 OR failed_inspections > 0 OR days_since_last_inspection > 60 ORDER BY critical_tasks DESC, failed_inspections DESC, days_since_last_inspection DESC LIMIT 720
